annual_revenue_diff_100_minus_90 = annual_revenue_100 - annual_revenue_90\n\n        meds_out.append(\n            {\n                \"medication\": med,\n ${absolute_total_revenue_difference:.2f}\",\n        f\"Final decision: {decision}\",\n    ]\n    md_path.write_text(\"\\n\".join(md_lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\nif __name__ ==
